Checked against CPIC 2022 and CPIC 2017 (full text via the NCBI efetch service), CPIC 2011 (abstract only; full text not accessible), Mazzeffi 2017, Jourdi 2022, Gragnano 2022, Bélanger 2020 and Lee 2014.
- **Inside or Outside the DRG: The Payment Window, APCs and the Lab Fee Schedule (Ch. 13):** 2 pending claims (the reviewer's cost-center/revenue-center framing; Number Vault figures not taught); lesson flags f-window-calendar (missing-context: the window is the admission day plus 3 calendar days for IPPS hospitals, or the admission day plus 1 day before for non-IPPS hospitals, not a 72-hour clock, and the lab date of service is the collection date, not the order date), f-opps-packaging (missing-context: since 2014 an OPPS hospital's outpatient lab tests are generally packaged, and are paid separately on the CLFS only when they are the only outpatient service that day), f-us-only-payment (guideline-dependent: US Medicare only); register flags rv-payment-window-calendar-days, rv-opps-lab-packaging. Checked against the eCFR text of 42 CFR 412.2, 412.60, 414.507, 414.510, 419.1, 419.2, 419.20, 419.21, 419.22 and 419.31 (version 2026-10-01), the CMS Three Day Payment Window and Clinical Laboratory Fee Schedule pages, and the CMS Medicare Claims Processing Manual chapter 16, section 30.3 (all read directly, no page-fetch tool). Independent verification corrected the lesson's claim that outpatient lab tests are generally paid on the fee schedule.
- **Immune Screens: TRECs, CH50 and SPEP Clonality (Ch. 44):** 1 pending claim (reviewer's "curative" transplant wording, not taught; lesson teaches the early-transplant survival advantage instead); 1 open lesson flag (f-ch50-consumption-trap, missing-context); 1 proposed register flag (rv-ch50-consumption-trap, Exam Traps item 3). Checked against van der Burg 2019 and Thakar 2017 (TRECs), Ekdahl 2018 (complement function and activation products), Pires 2025 and Noonan 2026 (SPEP, immunofixation, MGUS including the no-CRAB criterion), all read as Europe PMC full text; no page-fetch tool used. - **PCR Variants: Nested, Multiplex, Allele-Specific and Competitive PCR (Ch. 69):** 1 pending claim (IDH1/TERT glioma 0.1% examples); lesson flags f-nested-closed (missing-context), f-asp-sensitivity (method-dependent); register flags rv-nested-closed-tube-context, rv-allele-specific-sensitivity-assay. - **Daratumumab, DTT, Lectins and Inhibition (Ch. 40):** 1 pending claim (Hit 28 "ficin enhances P1", not verified and not taught); 2 open lesson flags (missing-context: DTT also denatures Lutheran/Yt/JMH/LW/Cromer/Indian/Dombrock/Knops antigens besides Kell; Dolichos biflorus also agglutinates Tn and Cad cells); proposed register flags rv-dtt-other-antigens and rv-dolichos-tn-cad. - **HLA Genes, Expression and Minor Antigens (Ch. 50):** 1 pending claim (class II chain weights 33–35/26–28 kDa and the exact 44-kDa heavy chain; class I absence on muscle and sperm; HLA-C “inefficient association + mutated enhancer A” mechanism); 2 lesson flags (f-hlag-tolerance-evidence uncertain, f-hla-c-mechanism uncertain); register flags rv-hlag-tolerance-evidence and rv-hla-c-expression-mechanism proposed. - **Fluorescence, Time-Resolved Europium and Chemiluminescence (Ch. 4):** 1 pending claim (the reviewer's exact lifetime figures, which are not taught: fluorescence 10⁻⁹–10⁻⁶ s, phosphorescence 10⁻⁴–10 s, Eu³⁺ 10–1000 μs, fluorescein 4.5 ns); 1 open lesson flag (f-luminescence-lifetimes, uncertain); 1 proposed register flag (rv-ch4-luminescence-lifetimes). - **Single Source of Truth, Denormalization and the LIS Schema (Ch. 12):** 1 pending claim (the reviewer's recommendation that the schema must be contractually secured at purchase; no consulted source states it as a standard requirement, so the lesson attributes it to the reviewer); 1 lesson flag (f-duplication-ri, missing-context: duplicated data is a consistency problem, not a referential-integrity violation in the strict foreign-key sense); register flag rv-duplication-vs-referential-integrity proposed. Checked against the PostgreSQL documentation (Section 5.5 Constraints; Appendix M Glossary), Dilling 2020 (Adv Radiat Oncol), Angles 2024 (Database (Oxford)) and Arvisais-Anhalt 2022 (J Pathol Inform), all read as Europe PMC full text or the documentation HTML; no page-fetch tool used. - **Choosing the Test: Paired t, Chi-Square vs Fisher, and Rank Tests (Ch. 10):** 0 pending claims; 4 open lesson flags (chi-square expected-count rule is ≥5 in at least 80% of cells and none below 1, not every cell; paired t gains power only with positively correlated pairs; rank-test switch omits large-sample tolerance and power loss; ±2 SD 95.5% vs 95.4% rounding); register flags rv-chi-square-expected-80pct, rv-paired-t-positive-correlation, rv-nonparametric-large-sample. - **Case Report or Research? - **siRNA vs miRNA and Epigenetic Silencing (Ch. 68):** teaches Hits 5 and 8 and Exam Trap 2 only (Table 2 / Do Not Confuse not claimed, since only its siRNA vs miRNA row is covered); 1 pending claim (reviewer's "both 22–26 nt, cut by Dicer into 21–24 nt" sizes and miRNA "bulging" wording, not taught); lesson flags f-mirna-repression-plus-decay (missing-context: miRNA also drives deadenylation-dependent mRNA decay, not translational blockade alone) and f-sirna-mirna-size (uncertain: sources give siRNA 21–23 nt, miRNA duplex 18–25 nt, mature average 22 nt); register flags rv-mirna-repression-plus-decay and rv-sirna-mirna-size-range. Checked against Lam 2015, O'Brien 2018 and Kondo 2009 (Europe PMC full text) and Moore 2013 (read via a page-fetch tool, first part of the page only); independently re-verified.
- **Antibiotic Targets, Synergy Testing and the Septic-Shock Clock (Ch. 58):** 1 pending claim (reviewer's ≥2-log10 time-kill wording, tolerance as bacteriologic failure despite susceptibility, rapid-AST rationale, static labels for clindamycin/linezolid). Lesson flags: f-kumar-context (missing-context), f-synergy-method (method-dependent), f-tolerance-def (uncertain). Register flags: rv-ch58-kumar-context, rv-ch58-synergy-method. Checked in open full text against Kapoor 2017, Gajdács 2017, Stein 2015, Cázares-Domínguez 2015, Kumar 2014 and the SSC 2021 guideline (Evans 2021). Kohanski 2010 and Doern 2014 were each read via a page-fetch tool. The Kumar 2006 original was not read. In an independent fact-check, the SSC timing statements were corrected (≤1 h for possible shock or likely sepsis; ≤3 h without shock only if concern persists), and the MBC/MIC ≥32 tolerance definition was scoped to vancomycin in MRSA.
- **Proving HLA Linkage: Type 1 Diabetes, Narcolepsy and the LOD Score (Ch. 51):** 1 pending claim (observed 60/35/5 HLA sharing in diabetic sib pairs and the reviewer's 15-25% DQB1*06:02 carrier range); lesson flags f-t1d-twin-concordance (missing-context: MZ concordance about 50% but 65% by age 60) and f-narcolepsy-type (missing-context: the NPV >99% applies to narcolepsy with cataplexy and is calculated from a case-control sample; only 30-50% of type 2 carry DQB1*06:02); register flags rv-t1d-twin-concordance and rv-narcolepsy-dqb1-type. Checked against Ott 2015, Noble 2011 and Tafti 2014 (all read via a page-fetch tool), Gorman 2012, Concannon 2009, Pociot 2010 and Miyagawa 2019; all seven citations re-verified against Europe PMC metadata.
- **Organ Signature Antibodies: TSH Receptor, Gastric, Insulin and Myasthenia Serology (Ch. 55):** 1 pending claim (exact 14-day IAA cut-off, exogenous insulin antibodies 'running much higher', PCA 55% in advanced disease, IFA blocking and binding types, transplacental neonatal MG; not taught); lesson flags f-mg-seronegative-fraction (method-dependent), f-graves-tsi-atrophic (missing-context) and f-iaa-induction-days (outdated); register flags rv-mg-seronegative-fraction, rv-graves-tsi-atrophic-coexist and rv-iaa-insulin-induction-days. Checked against Kahaly and Diana 2017, Diana 2018, Furmaniak 2013, Vargas-Uricoechea 2023, Rustgi 2021, Andres and Serraj 2012, Iwamuro 2023, Kawasaki 2023, Sacks 2011 and the Sacks 2023 update (read via a page-fetch tool, islet and insulin antibody sections only, after the Europe PMC full text and the PMC page were both unavailable) and two Lazaridis and Tzartos 2020 reviews; all twelve citations re-verified against Europe PMC.
- **Serous Fluid Traps: Pericardial Cutoffs, Low Pleural Glucose, Secondary Peritonitis and Postpericardiotomy Syndrome (Ch. 30):** 1 pending claim (pericardial protein >3.0 g/dL 22% specificity, PPS fluid pH 7.35–7.45 with normal glucose, ascitic Gram stain ~25% sensitivity, SBP >92% monomicrobial); lesson flags on Light's criteria in pericardial fluid, diuretic misclassification, PPS pH/glucose and SBP Gram-stain figures; register flags rv-pericardial-light-criteria, rv-lights-diuretic-misclassification, rv-pps-fluid-ph-glucose, rv-sbp-gram-stain-figures. Table 2 is not claimed as taught (SAAG and fluid-clue rows not covered). Checked against Milevoj Kopcinovic & Culej 2014, Ege 2006, Malik 2021, Gabaldo 2019, Sinha 2016, Karkhanis & Joshi 2012, Rodríguez-Zarco 2019, Park & Choi 2020, KASL 2018, Aithal 2021 (BSG) and Lu 2017 (all Europe PMC full text; no page-fetch tool used); independently fact-checked, which corrected the Ege 2006 cohort description and removed an unsourced PPS timing figure.
- **Patient Blood Management: Fix Anaemia, One Unit at a Time, and the Iron Bill (Ch. 41):** maps only High-Yield Hits 24 and 26 (the Number Vault table ch41.table1 is not claimed, because only its first row's dose rule is used); 1 pending claim (Number Vault 70-kg qualifier and 'Hct range 2–9%', not taught); lesson flags: chelation-not-phlebotomy needs post-HSCT context (missing-context), 'no excretion' means no excretion of excess iron (missing-context), single-unit/threshold rules are NICE/international guideline-dependent (Philippine practice not covered); register flags rv-iron-phlebotomy-after-cure, rv-iron-no-excretion. Independent check fixed the functional-iron-deficiency criterion (inflammation OR TSAT <20%), the pillar 3 wording (patient-centred decisions, not thresholds), an 'only chelation' overstatement and unsupported comparison points. Checked against SIR4PBM (Meybohm 2017), NICE NG24 red-cell chapter (read from the NICE website), Krishna 2023, Saliba 2015, Kwiatkowski 2011 and Pinyopornpanish 2023 (all read in full text via Europe PMC).
- **Kidney-Cleared Drugs: Lithium, Aminoglycosides and Methotrexate (Ch. 24):** 2 pending claims (reviewer lithium tier wording 1.5–2.5 slight–moderate / 2.5–3.5 severe / >3.5 potentially lethal; leucovorin start as early as 18 h); 3 lesson flags (f-lithium-tiers, f-mtx-leucovorin, f-gent-trough); register flags rv-lithium-tiers-extrip, rv-mtx-leucovorin-window, rv-gentamicin-trough-current-practice. Checked against Decker 2015 (EXTRIP lithium; read via a page-fetch tool, first ~100,000 of ~185,000 characters only), Liu 2022, Erden 2013, Sheikh 2022, Gitlin 2016, Howard 2016, Bielack 2024, Moore 2025 (Europe PMC full text) and the US gentamicin prescribing information (DailyMed SPL, revised 10/2022). Independently fact-checked: citations re-verified; 'many centres' prevalence wording and the 'coma' paraphrase of EXTRIP corrected; the reviewer's <50 nM methotrexate end-point found consistent with Howard 2016 (0.05 µmol/L) and removed from the flagged claim.
- **Acute Pancreatitis: Clues to Cause and Ranson Severity (Ch. 23):** 1 pending claim (urine amylase > 1000 Somogyi units/hour; AST-alone > 3× ULN gallstone marker; lipase/amylase ratio > 5 cut-off); 4 lesson flags (ALT gallstone sensitivity, lipase/amylase ratio cut-off, biliary Ranson version, urine amylase in late presenters); register flags rv-ap-alt-gallstone-sensitivity, rv-ap-lipase-amylase-ratio-cutoff, rv-ranson-biliary-version, rv-urine-amylase-late-presenters. Checked against Shah 2018, Hazem 2009, Ekka 2023 (pooled sensitivity 74%, specificity 63% from its Results; its Discussion restates 75%/56%), McCarrick 2025, Abu-Eshy 2008, Lee 2022, Hu 2023 and Skude 2000 (all full text via Europe PMC; no page-fetch tool used). Independent re-check corrected the lipase/amylase accuracy figures.
- **Hepatitis A, Hepatitis C and Wilson Disease: Markers That Mislead (Ch. 22):** 1 pending claim (reviewer's 15%/35% normal-ceruloplasmin figures, not taught); lesson flags f-wd-ceruloplasmin-percent (uncertain), f-hav-relapse (missing-context), f-hcv-chronicity-range (method-dependent); register flags rv-wilson-ceruloplasmin-normal-percent, rv-hav-relapsing-igm-context. Checked against ACIP 2020 hepatitis A (Nelson), CDC 2020 HCV screening (Schillie), Chen and Morgan 2006, Uto 2012, the AASLD 2023 Wilson summary (Alkhouri), Schroeder 2021, Penning 2023 and Seo 2012, all read as Europe PMC full text (no page-fetch tool used). Independent check softened 'always resolves' (hepatitis A: never chronic, rarely fulminant) and 'modest ALT is not a mild prognosis' (now: does not guarantee mild disease).
- **Measuring Enzyme Activity: Units, NADH at 340 nm and the LD Direction (Ch. 21):** 2 pending claims (Hit 13 temperature rule of doubling per 10 °C and ±0.1 °C, not taught; reviewer's 'most enzyme assays are NADH assays', softened to 'several common assays, such as ALT and LD'); lesson flags f-ld-direction-reason (missing-context) and f-temp-rule-unverified (uncertain); register flag rv-ld-direction-rationale. Checked against the BIPM SI Brochure (CGPM 1999 Resolution 12), Infusino 2009 and Siekmann 2001 (EJIFCC), Sun 2021, Hu 2026, Robinson 2015, Rendón 2025, Ramsay 2017, Jońca 2015, Hata 2022, the IFCC LD method (Bais 1994; abstract only, full-text PDF blocked) and Voma 2016 ADLM Pearls (read via a page-fetch tool).
- **Transferrin’s Many Faces: Saturation, CDT, β2-Transferrin and Wilson Disease (Ch. 20):** 1 pending claim (reviewer’s serum-alongside/allelic-variant advice for β2-transferrin and the “>650 ATP7B mutations” figure, neither taught); 4 open lesson flags (TSAT paired with ferritin; ATP7B variant count and ceruloplasmin limits; CDT false-negative groups; β2-transferrin not CSF-only); register flags rv-hh-tsat-with-ferritin, rv-atp7b-variant-count, rv-cdt-false-negative-groups (needs verification), rv-b2-transferrin-ch20-qualifiers. Checked against Bacon 2011 (AASLD hemochromatosis guideline), Rohr 2023, Fagan 2014, Oh 2019, Oh 2017, Torabi 2024 and Alkhouri 2023 (AASLD Wilson summary), all read as Europe PMC full text and re-read by an independent check, which removed an unsupported “tetrasialotransferrin dominates” statement and made the β2-transferrin case conditional on a positive result.
- **Low Calcium, High PTH: CKD, PTH Assays and PTH Resistance (Ch. 16):** 1 pending claim (assay-specific intact and biointact PTH reference intervals, not taught); lesson flags f-biointact-essential (missing-context), f-php-infusion (missing-context), f-pth-interval (method-dependent); register flags rv-biointact-pth-routine, rv-php-camp-infusion. Checked against KDIGO 2017 CKD-MBD guideline, Kritmetapak 2019, Chandran 2019, Salera 2025, Mantovani 2018 (PHP consensus) and Albert 2025 (all full text via Europe PMC; no page-fetch tool used). Independent check removed unsupported 'use one assay' advice and corrected the first-generation assay and fragment-share wording.
- **Sigma Metrics, Power Function Graphs and QC Targets (Ch. 11):** 1 pending claim (the reviewer's power-function detection figures for 1_2s, 1_3s and 1_3s/2_2s, and 'long-term SD cumulative over 6–12 months, never the average of monthly SDs'); lesson flag f-12s-n (missing-context: 1_2s false-rejection rate depends on the number of control levels per run); register flag rv-12s-false-rejection-n. Checked against Westgard, Bayat and Westgard 2018 and 2019, Gruber 2024, Giannoli 2021, Ricós 2022 and Costa-Pallaruelo 2025 (all Europe PMC full text; CLSI C24 itself not read, taught as reported by Gruber and Giannoli). Independent check: citations confirmed; fixed a prerequisite cycle (now ch10.hit9, ch2.hit4), removed the unsupported 'simulation' wording and qualified 'no 1_2s rejection by default' as the review authors' advice.
- **When a Bedside Result Needs Backup: Urine hCG, Fingerstick Creatinine and Cardiac Markers (Ch. 7):** 1 pending claim (QuickVue 25 mIU/mL specification, 10–20 specimen dipstick validation size, “not ideal” fingerstick quote, Number Vault figures); lesson flags f-dipstick-validation-size (guideline-dependent), f-poc-cardiac-efficiency (missing-context), f-us-only-poctlim (guideline-dependent); register flags rv-ch7-dipstick-validation-size, rv-ch7-poc-cardiac-efficiency. Checked against Gnoth and Johnson 2014 (read via a page-fetch tool), Priyadarshini 2022, Winder 2017, Nataatmadja 2020, Lemoine 2022, the RATPAC trial (Goodacre 2011; abstracts only), Rooney and Schilling 2014, Shaw 2016, Khan 2019, and 42 CFR 493.15, 493.1200 and 493.1253 (eCFR 2026-10-01). Independent fact-check corrected the Lemoine 84% figure (measured GFR < 60), RATPAC discharge definition and non-significant cost difference, and softened dilution and outcome wording.
- **Herpesvirus Edge Cases: HSV Encephalitis PCR, the Lonely VCA IgG and Return to Sport (Ch. 64):** 1 pending claim (HSV-specific CSF culture sensitivity and turnaround, and the 'fatal' wording of Hit 12, taught only in source wording); lesson flag f-im-return-to-play (missing-context); register flag rv-im-return-to-play. Checked against Bradshaw 2016, Venkatesan 2013 and De Paschale 2012 (each read via a page-fetch tool), Simon 2013, Balfour 2015 and Becker 2014; independent verification corrected PCR figures, three question options/rationales and return-to-play wording.
- **Rapid Fungal Diagnosis: Cryptococcal Antigen, T2Candida and Fungal PCR (Ch. 60):** 2 pending claims (T2Candida 1–3 CFU/mL detection limit and 4.2 h vs >120 h time to negativity; ITS sequencing of non-sporulating molds), not taught; 3 open lesson flags (India ink sensitivity burden/population-dependent, T2Candida five-species coverage and pooled specificity, Pneumocystis PCR colonization); register flags rv-ch60-india-ink-sensitivity-burden, rv-ch60-t2candida-five-species, rv-ch60-pjp-pcr-colonization. Checked against Boulware 2014, Skipper 2019, Kwizera 2024, Mylonakis 2018 (STAMP), Tang 2019, Wickes 2018, Fan 2013 and Filippidis 2026 (all full text via Europe PMC; no page-fetch tool used); independent re-check corrected 'pivotal trial' wording, an overstated blood-culture point, unsourced example species, and added a source for the culture-for-susceptibility statement.
- **Planning Automation: 90th-Percentile Turnaround Goals and Master Curves (Ch. 6):** 2 pending claims (the reviewer's 30/45/20/30-minute RFP targets, shown only as one lab's example; the Hit 12 RFP/site-visit/finalist/6-month sequence and master-lot label contents); lesson flags f-tat-local (uncertain), f-stat-priority (missing-context), f-master-curve (method-dependent), f-plan-seq (uncertain); register flags rv-ch6-tat-targets-local, rv-ch6-stat-priority-tla, rv-ch6-master-curve-local-calibration, rv-ch6-tla-planning-sequence. Checked against Hawkins 2007 and Armbruster 2014 (both read via a page-fetch tool), Ellison 2018, Archetti 2017, Nam & Park 2025, Mahler 2016, Lijia 2020 and Fortunato 2016; independent fact-check softened the Hawkins 60-minute figure to a suggested starting point and 'no universal goals' to 'universal goals difficult to recommend'.
- **TB Transmission and Control: Droplet Nuclei, Isolation Rooms, N95s and MAC Risk in HIV (Ch. 59):** 1 pending claim (the reviewer's 12/hour autopsy figure as a number, and prophylaxis doses; neither taught as fact); lesson flags for droplet-nucleus size (1–5 µm, not 1–10), the yearly TB risk with HIV (review range), AII air changes (≥12 new or renovated, ≥6 older rooms), the N95 0.3 µm specification, MAC prophylaxis with prompt ART, and US-only infection-control guidance; register flags rv-tb-droplet-nuclei-size, rv-tb-hiv-annual-risk, rv-tb-aii-air-changes, rv-tb-respirator-filter-spec, rv-mac-prophylaxis-art. Checked against the CDC 2005 health-care TB guideline (Jensen 2005), the CDC TB and latent-TB clinical overview pages, Gopalaswamy 2020, Migliori 2021, Pawlowski 2012, the Southern African HIV Clinicians Society NTM guideline (Dawood 2024) and To 2020. All were read directly, with no page-fetch tool needed. The US OI guideline page returned 403 and was not read. An independent fact-check re-read every source and qualified the takeaway and HEPA wording.
- **Anti-GBM, Double Positives and ANCA-Negative Vasculitis (Ch. 54):** 1 pending claim (reviewer's 11–17%/9–16% seronegativity figures, “capillaritis”/“myocarditis without vasculitis” EGPA wording, Number Vault anti-GBM incidence and assay figures); lesson flag f-gbm-creatinine (missing-context: no 5 mg/dL treatment cutoff in the source); register flag rv-anti-gbm-creatinine-cutoff. Checked against McAdoo and Pusey 2025 (ERA anti-GBM treatment standard), Kozmar 2025 (Croatian ANCA testing recommendations), Lyons 2019 (EGPA GWAS by ANCA status), Weng and Liu 2019 (drug-induced AAV) and Jin 2018 (levamisole-adulterated cocaine), all read as Europe PMC full text; independent fact-check softened overstated dual-ANCA and assay-sensitivity wording and replaced unsourced distractor rationales.
- **Beyond the ANA: Centromere, Topoisomerase I, Ro/La and U1RNP (Ch. 53):** 1 pending claim (reviewer subset percentages for anti-centromere/anti-Scl-70, anti-centromere in 25% of idiopathic Raynaud, "all 20 original Sharp cases", MCTD evolving "most often" to scleroderma, CREST letters); lesson flags f-ssc-aca-ata-context (missing-context), f-mctd-sharp-evolution (contradiction), f-isolated-la-assay (method-dependent); register flags rv-ssc-aca-ata-context, rv-mctd-sharp-evolution, rv-isolated-la-assay. - **Complement Triggers and Alarms: C1q, IgG Subclasses, MBL and Anaphylatoxins (Ch. 48):** 1 pending claim (exact typical MBL level of 1.5 µg/mL and printed range 0.002–10 µg/mL; C4a as an equal anaphylatoxin; complete inactivation by desArg conversion), not taught; 3 open lesson flags (C4a activity debated and C5a desArg weakened not inactive; IgG2 a weak C1q binder; MBL-deficiency prevalence definition-dependent); register flags rv-c4a-anaphylatoxin-debated, rv-igg2-weak-c1q and rv-mbl-deficiency-definition. Checked against Merle 2015 (Front Immunol), Vidarsson 2014 (Front Immunol), Thiel 2006 (Mol Immunol), Dahl 2004 (J Exp Med) and Auriti 2017 (J Immunol Res), all read as Europe PMC full text; independent fact-check corrected the IgG3 hinge description (long, not flexible), histamine production wording, and the size of the acute-phase MBL rise (sources disagree: 3–4-fold vs small).
- **Chemokine Names, Receptors and Measuring Soluble Immune Signals (Ch. 49):** 1 pending claim (bioassay as “gold standard”, bioassay cost, chemokine 8–14 kDa); lesson flags f-xc-two-cysteines, f-ackr-receptors, f-bioassay-gold-standard, f-psel-research-marker; register flags rv-xc-chemokine-cysteines, rv-atypical-chemokine-receptors, rv-cytokine-bioassay-gold-standard, rv-soluble-p-selectin-dvt. Checked against Hughes and Nibbs 2018 and Xavier 2025 (full text via Europe PMC), and Bachelerie 2014, Stenken 2015, Leng 2008 and Ramacciotti 2011 (read via a page-fetch tool; Bachelerie and Stenken only the first part of the page). - **NK Cells, the Oxidative Burst, Cytokine Types and Age-Matched Ranges (Ch. 46):** 1 pending claim (NBT ~10%/80% reduction figures, IL-4 specifically suppressing IFN-γ–producing cells, type 2 cytokines 'driving B-cell differentiation', ranges hard to set in the first year, NK 'reduced/absent' at birth); lesson flags f-nbt-superseded (outdated), f-peds-nk (method-dependent) and f-th2-tfh (missing-context: B-cell help now credited mainly to Tfh cells); register flags rv-nbt-superseded-dhr, rv-pediatric-nk-few-method-dependent and rv-th2-bcell-help-tfh. Checked against Hu 2019, Abel 2018, Vojdani 2024 and Wu 2020 (NK cells), Berger 2000 and Zhang & An 2007 (both read via a page-fetch tool) and Lee 2023 (cytokine types, Tfh), Yu 2018 and Arnold 2017 (NBT/DHR, CGD), and Zhang 2022, Besci 2021 and Lerkvaleekul 2020 (paediatric lymphocyte subsets). An independent check corrected a misread Zhang 2022 verification statistic.
- **Immunoassay Labels: From Insulin RIA to Chemiluminescence, ECLIA and the HBsAg Cutoff Index (Ch. 45):** 1 pending claim (reviewer RIA ~5 pg/mL and CL-EIA ~0.1 pg/mL sensitivity figures, a "limited" antibody amount in competitive RIA, the ECLIA "gold" electrode, and the 0.5 mIU/mL / 8.0 SD HBsAg cutoff); lesson flag f-hbsag-cutoff-assay-specific; register flag rv-hbsag-cutoff-assay-specific. Checked against Ahsan 2022, Cinquanta 2017, Zhang 2019, Vogeser 2014, Park 2021, Lee 2023, Zhang 2022 and Wang 2022 (all full text via Europe PMC; no page-fetch tool used). Taught as about 620 nm for the ECLIA emission (Zhang 2022, Wang 2022); Zhang 2019 gives 642 nm. Independent fact-check: citations match Europe PMC metadata; RIA competitive principle reworded to drop the unsupported "limited antibody" detail; CLIA sensitivity advantage qualified as reported for enhanced peroxidase chemistry.
- **Donor Safety at the Chair: Blood Volume, Circuit Limits, Reactions and Draw Time (Ch. 42):** 1 pending claim (AABB attribution of the 10.5 mL/kg circuit limit; taught only as 15% of 70 mL/kg); lesson flags for the draw-time rule (guideline-dependent), the pulse fork (missing-context) and the unverified 10.5 mL/kg attribution (uncertain, US/AABB only); register flags rv-draw-time-component-limits (needs-verification: the only open source is a case report summarising earlier literature) and rv-vasovagal-pulse-clue. Checked against Potente 2020 (Gilcher’s rule of fives), Basu 2014, Philip 2013, Philip 2014, Basavarajegowda 2024 and Rajkumar 2024 (all Europe PMC full text), then independently re-checked; the UK Red Book page redirected and could not be read, and the standard behind the draw-time limits (a 2021 Transfus Clin Biol article) was not open access.
- **Reagent Water, pH and Normality (Ch. 4):** 1 pending claim (CLSI/CAP > 10 MΩ·cm Type I specification, the reviewer's 95–99% RO removal figure and 'most common contaminant' wording); lesson flags f-type-i-terminology (outdated), f-ro-removal (method-dependent), f-kw-temperature and f-normality-reaction (missing-context), f-us-standards-water (guideline-dependent); register flags rv-ch4-type-i-water-clrw (needs-verification), rv-ch4-kw-neutral-ph-temperature, rv-ch4-normality-reaction-dependent. - **B Cells in Disguise: Myeloma-Defining Events, Waldenström MYD88 and the Hodgkin Cell (Ch. 34):** written and source-referenced, but the independent fact-check has NOT yet been completed; treat as unverified until this line is updated.
- **When Tests Disagree: Lupus Anticoagulant, LAHPS and Bleeding with a Normal Screen (Ch. 36):** 1 pending claim (LA 'mostly non-SLE', 2–5 h euglobulin lysis range, α2-antiplasmin concentration and 24-h non-lysis statement not taught); 4 lesson flags (LA non-SLE share uncertain, LAHPS treatment context, normal-screen differential, hyperfibrinolysis test method); register flags rv-ch36-la-non-sle-share, rv-ch36-lahps-context, rv-ch36-normal-screen-ddx, rv-ch36-hyperfibrinolysis-tests. Independent check: LAHPS mixing pattern narrowed to the single reported case, question rationales corrected (factor VIII inhibitors can mimic LA in confirm tests), hyperfibrinolysis marked rare.
- **Whole-Blood Donor Eligibility:** 1 pending claim (age, 10-minute collection, directed donors; not taught); 1 register flag (Hits 15–16). US FDA rules only; Philippine donor criteria not checked.
- **β-Lactamases (ESBL, AmpC, CPE):** no pending claims; 2 register flags (Trap 5 aztreonam; Hit 5 nitrocefin, needs verification). Nitrocefin colour/timing and the temocillin OXA-48 screen are not taught.
- **Troponin and Myocardial Injury:** 1 pending claim (sepsis as a cause; not taught); 1 register flag (Fifth UDMI). Numeric ng/L thresholds and type 4a/5 multiples are not taught.
- **Hemophilia in the Lab:** no pending claims; 1 register flag on Hit 5 (mild range). Prevalence, bleed frequencies, intron 22 and inhibitor percentages are not taught; the source gives the Bethesda detection limit as 0.42 BU/mL (reviewer: ~0.5).
- **Choosing the Component:** 1 pending claim (platelets in ITP/TTP; CCI < 7,500 definition; neither taught); 2 register flags (Hit 5 acute-MI trigger; Hit 7 cryo trigger). Per-unit increments and the 100 × 10⁹/L CNS platelet threshold are not taught.
- **Tick-Borne Rickettsial Diseases:** 1 pending claim (Weil–Felix obsolete, < 20% seropositive at presentation, Coxiella by inhalation; none taught); 1 register flag on Trap 5. Q fever chronic cut-off taught as CDC ≥ 1:1024 with the Duke > 1:800 criterion (reviewer: ≥ 1:800).
- **Pancreatic Enzymes:** 1 pending claim (lipase 8–14-day duration; not taught); 1 register flag on Hit 2 (diagnostic criteria). The reviewer’s 48–72-h amylase normalisation differs from the source read (3–5 days) and is not taught. Choi 2023 is in Korean; only its English tables were used.
- **Hemolytic Anemia Workup:** 1 pending claim (splenectomy rationale in HS; Ham test as historical; neither taught); 1 register flag on Hit 5 (osmotic fragility).
- **DAT Patterns and AIHA Workup:** 1 pending claim (IgM dissociation leaving C3d; post-viral timing of PCH; neither taught); no flags. The reviewer’s “70–80% of AIHA” warm figure and CAD thermal amplitude are not taught.
- **Chlamydia and Mycoplasma:** no pending claims; 2 register flags (Hit 3 energy metabolism; Trap 4 regimen, not taught). EB/RB sizes are not taught.
- **Dynamic Endocrine Testing:** 1 pending claim (the CBG mechanism behind estrogen-related DST false positives); 1 register flag on Hit 1 (acromegaly diagnosis). The GHD cut-off (3 µg/L) is not taught because thresholds are assay-dependent.
- **Erythropoiesis and RPI:** 1 pending claim (shift-reticulocyte mechanism and ~1-day normal maturation); no flags. Notes that labs band hematocrit differently for maturation time, and that the reviewer’s ⁵¹Cr and 3-million-cells-per-second figures are not taught.
- **Component Storage and Irradiation:** 1 pending claim (RBC transport 1–10 °C, the US 5-day thawed-plasma relabel, never irradiating HPCs; none stated as numbers); 2 register flags on Hit 10 (5-day platelet storage, verified; pH ≥ 6.0, needs verification). Irradiation dose taught as the US FDA rule, with the Council of Europe range noted.
- **hCG and PUL:** 1 pending claim (progesterone ng/mL cut-offs); 2 register flags on Hits 1–2.
- **Susceptibility testing (MIC, breakpoints, D-test):** 1 pending claim (current CLSI wording for reporting clindamycin as resistant after a positive D-test, and exact broth-microdilution conditions); no flags. Breakpoint numbers are deliberately not taught because they change with each CLSI M100 edition.
- **Flow Cytometry Gating:** 1 pending claim (hemodilution/lysis explanation); no flags. Adds the genetic AML exceptions to the 20% blast threshold.
- **Antibody ID — Dosage and Kidd:** 1 pending claim (DHTR 5–10 days); 1 register flag (S/s enzyme effect, needs verification). Adds Rh (non-D) to the dosage list.
- **Syphilis Serology:** no pending claims; no flags. Notes CDC’s 15–25% treponemal seroreversion after primary-stage treatment (reviewer: “up to 20%”) and the “lipoidal antigen” terminology.
- **LDL Calculation:** no pending claims; 1 register flag on Trap 2.
